## 3. Candidate-token inventory (never exact-once; unique keys; at-most-one defined/named per page/raw token)

**Descriptor:** candidate-token inventory for the sixteen pages at basis 61cacb2d. Keys are unique (page,line,class,raw token); no duplicate mentions; at most one defined/named disposition per (page,raw token). Counts come from the FINAL table below. Class rules: i uppercase/coded identifiers/acronyms/roles; ii lowercase technical/audience prose; iii non-backticked path/identifier; iv whole backticked code/command/identifier, or raw 40/12-hex SHA/commit hash; v statuses, dates, line-ranges, ranges, placeholders, operators. Disposition codes: defined at first use, named gap - first use before any expansion on this page, or documented exclusion - CODE: reason with legend DATE, LINE-RANGE, STATUS, RANGE, PLACEHOLDER, OPERATOR, PROSE, EMPHASIS, PATH, COMMAND, SHA, LITERAL. Status tokens ACCEPT/ACCEPTED and variants are class v; operator glyphs incl U+21D4/U+2194 are class v OPERATOR; SHA/commit hashes are class iv SHA; H/M/L/P placeholders class v PLACEHOLDER; all-caps common words class i EMPHASIS; coded IDs class i named/defined; lowercase json/api prose class ii PROSE; DATE/LINE-RANGE class v.

## 5. Whole-page B1-B6 evidence per page

**Canonical acceptance B1-B6 (verbatim from accepted block-A plan r1 comment 6278341224; byte-identical in block-B plan r4 comment 6279405619):**
~~~text
- Open with what the page or component is for and who should read it.
- Define an abbreviation or coded identifier at first use. For example,
  “Behavioral Eval Runner (BER)” and “work package (WP)” are explained before
  later shorthand. Explain lifecycle/status codes in plain words or link to
  a nearby glossary; do not assume a reader knows `CP-WP-003` (control-plane
  work package 003), `OD-1` (owner decision 1), `T03` (transition 03), or
  internal class names.
- Use descriptive headings, short paragraphs, lists or tables for distinct
  functions, and at least one concrete example where a component's purpose
  would otherwise remain abstract. Explain what a function or command accepts,
  produces, changes, and refuses when those details matter to safe use.
- State shipped behavior separately from proposed or blocked behavior. A
  synthetic test, passing check or approved design is not described as a
  deployed integration or measured model result.
- Keep navigation links current and check that every referenced file or
  command exists. Preserve the technical contract while making the prose
  approachable; do not remove a safety boundary for brevity.
- Obtain a read-only review from someone who did not write the page. Ask the
  reviewer to explain the component's purpose and normal use from the page
  alone, and correct gaps before marking that batch complete.
~~~

~~~text
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json,sys,os
P=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__),"Crows-472.json")
def main(a):
 d=json.load(open(P,encoding="utf-8"))
 if not a or a[0]=="path": print(P); return 0
 if a[0]=="rows": print(json.dumps(d["rows"],indent=1,ensure_ascii=False)); return 0
 if a[0]=="query" and len(a)>1:
  q=a[1]; m=[r for r in d["rows"] if q in r["skill"] or q in r["apply"] or q in r["result"]]
  print(json.dumps(m,indent=1,ensure_ascii=False)); return 0 if m else 1
 print("usage: preview-472.py [path|rows|query <text>]"); return 2
if __name__=="__main__": sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
~~~

## 9. PROC-04 method

The body publisher derives PROC-04 from the FINAL live PR body by: take whole-line witness/skills/security sentinel fields, whitespace-collapse each, join with LF, SHA-256 UTF-8; publish short+full digest and normalized lens; after ALL body edits recompute from the live body and ensure published==recomputed literally. Body checker must exit0. The record is canonical for counts/captures; the body binds plan/head/record facts.

## 10. Embedded self-contained fail-safe verifier (extract and run)

Extraction/execution: copy the single fenced python block below to verifier-472.py and run python verifier-472.py <this-record.md>. No third-party dependency. Exit0 prints the expected line; failures print VERIFIER FAIL and exit1; no argument exits2. **Scope note:** this verifier validates table-level self-consistency and capture-vs-disk hash equality for the retained artifacts; the repository facts (page blobs/bytes/LF, basis commit, one-added-path, plan bindings) are independently measured in this record and the PR body, not asserted by the verifier, whose embedded artifact hashes are internal-consistency checks and not a trust boundary for executing extracted code.

<!-- verifier:start -->
~~~python
import io,re,json,hashlib,subprocess,sys,os,shutil
if len(sys.argv)!=2:
    print('usage: python verifier-472.py <record.md>'); sys.exit(2)
record=sys.argv[1]
try: text=io.open(record,encoding='utf-8').read()
except Exception as e: print('VERIFIER FAIL: cannot read record:',e); sys.exit(2)
errors=[]
pre=text.split('<!-- verifier:start -->\n~~~python')[0]
def err(m): errors.append(m)
# inventory section
try:
 s=text.index('## 3. Candidate-token inventory'); e=text.index('## 4. Counts',s)
except ValueError:
 print('VERIFIER FAIL: inventory section missing'); sys.exit(1)
lines=text[s:e].split('\n')
rows=[];malformed=0
for l in lines:
    if not l.startswith('| P') or l.startswith('| Page '): continue
    # split respecting backslash escapes
    cells=[];cur='';i=0
    while i<len(l):
        ch=l[i]
        if ch=='\\' and i+1<len(l):
            nxt=l[i+1]
            if nxt in ('|','\\'): cur+=nxt;i+=2;continue
            cur+=ch;i+=1;continue
        if ch=='|': cells.append(cur.strip());cur='';i+=1;continue
        cur+=ch;i+=1
    cells=[c for c in cells if c!='']
    if len(cells)!=5:
        malformed+=1;continue
    pg,loc,cls,tok,disp=cells
    m=re.fullmatch(r'(\d+)@(\d+)',loc)
    if not pg.startswith('P') or not m or cls not in ('i','ii','iii','iv','v'):
        malformed+=1;err('bad row '+l[:180]);continue
    rows.append((pg,int(m.group(1)),int(m.group(2)),cls,tok,disp))
keys=set((r[0],r[1],r[4]) for r in rows)
from collections import Counter,defaultdict
classes=Counter(r[3] for r in rows)
disp=Counter('defined' if r[5].startswith('defined') else 'named' if r[5].startswith('named') else 'excluded' for r in rows)
per=defaultdict(lambda:[0,0,0,0,0]);pdisp=defaultdict(Counter)
for r in rows:
    per[r[0]][['i','ii','iii','iv','v'].index(r[3])]+=1
    pdisp[r[0]][('defined' if r[5].startswith('defined') else 'named' if r[5].startswith('named') else 'excluded')]+=1
if len(rows)!=5274: err('rows %d != 5274'%len(rows))
if len(keys)!=5274: err('unique keys %d != 5274'%len(keys))
if malformed: err('malformed rows %d'%malformed)
if dict(classes)!=dict(i=698,ii=68,iii=97,iv=2943,v=1468): err('classes mismatch '+repr(dict(classes)))
if dict(disp)!=dict(defined=10,named=658,excluded=4606): err('dispositions mismatch '+repr(dict(disp)))
# duplicate defined/named per page/token
seen=defaultdict(int)
for r in rows:
    if r[5].startswith(('defined','named')): seen[(r[0],r[4])]+=1
for k,v in seen.items():
    if v>1: err('duplicate defined/named token %r'% (k,))
# backtick class rule
BT=chr(96)
for r in rows:
    tok=r[4]
    if tok.startswith(BT) and tok.endswith(BT) and r[3] not in ('iv','v'): err('backticked non-iv/v '+repr(r))
    if ('<' in tok or '>' in tok) and not (tok.startswith(BT) and tok.endswith(BT)) and r[5].startswith('named'): err('raw angle named gap '+repr(r))
# codes sum
codes=Counter()
for r in rows:
    if r[5].startswith('documented exclusion - '):
        m=re.match(r'documented exclusion - ([A-Z-]+):',r[5])
        if not m: err('bad exclusion code '+repr(r))
        else: codes[m.group(1)]+=1
if sum(codes.values())!=disp['excluded']: err('code sum mismatch %d'%sum(codes.values()))
_exp_codes={'DATE':112,'RANGE':122,'STATUS':83,'SHA':63,'PATH':782,'PROSE':68,'LINE-RANGE':1093,'LITERAL':1382,'EMPHASIS':650,'COMMAND':193,'PLACEHOLDER':50,'OPERATOR':8}
if dict(codes)!=_exp_codes: err('exclusion code counts differ from expected matrix')
# per-page table
try:
 s2=text.index('## 4. Counts'); e2=text.index('## 5. Whole-page',s2)
except ValueError: err('counts section missing'); s2=e2=0
plines=[l for l in text[s2:e2].split('\n') if l.startswith('| P4')]
if len(plines)!=16: err('per-page count table rows %d'%len(plines))
for l in plines:
    c=[x.strip() for x in l.strip('|').split('|')];pg=c[0]
    try: vals=[int(x) for x in c[1:]]
    except: err('bad per-page row');continue
    expect=[sum(per[pg])]+per[pg]+[pdisp[pg].get('defined',0),pdisp[pg].get('named',0),pdisp[pg].get('excluded',0)]
    if vals!=expect: err('per-page mismatch '+pg+' '+repr(vals)+' != '+repr(expect))
# page total arithmetic check
# page total arithmetic check (byte/LF rows must sum to declared totals)
try:
 pt=[l for l in text[:text.index('## 3.')].split(chr(10)) if l.startswith('| P4')]
 if len(pt)!=16: err('page rows %d != 16'%len(pt))
 pb=plf=0
 for l in pt:
  cells=[c.strip() for c in l.strip('|').split('|')]
  if len(cells)<6: err('bad page row '+l[:40]); continue
  m=re.fullmatch(r'([\d,]+) / ([\d,]+)',cells[-1])
  if not m: err('bad page bytes/lf '+cells[-1]); continue
  pb+=int(m.group(1).replace(',','')); plf+=int(m.group(2).replace(',',''))
 if (pb,plf)!=(787102,7528): err('page totals %d/%d != 787102/7528'%(pb,plf))
 tl=[l for l in text.split(chr(10)) if l.startswith('| **Totals**')]
 if not any('787,102 / 7,528' in x for x in tl): err('pilot Totals line not 787,102/7,528')
except Exception as e: err('page totals arithmetic: '+str(e))


# section 4 tally matrix check (must agree with parsed inventory)
try:
 trows=[l for l in text.split(chr(10)) if l.startswith('| **Total** |')]
 if len(trows)!=1: err('section4 Total rows %d != 1'%len(trows))
 else:
  tc=[c.strip() for c in trows[0].strip('|').split('|')]
  vals=[int(x.replace(',','').replace('*','')) for x in tc[1:]]
  expect=[5274,698,68,97,2943,1468,10,658,4606]
  if vals!=expect: err('section4 matrix '+repr(vals)+' != '+repr(expect))
  if classes.get('i')!=698 or classes.get('ii')!=68 or classes.get('iii')!=97 or classes.get('iv')!=2943 or classes.get('v')!=1468: err('section4 class parsed mismatch')
  if disp.get('defined')!=10 or disp.get('named')!=658 or disp.get('excluded')!=4606: err('section4 disp parsed mismatch')
  if sum(codes.values())!=4606: err('section4 codes parsed mismatch')
  _sec4 = text[text.index('## 4. Counts'):text.index('## 5.')]
  if '698+68+97+2943+1468=5274' not in _sec4: err('class sum line missing')
  if '10+658+4606=5274' not in _sec4: err('disposition sum line missing')
  if '= 4606' not in _sec4: err('codes sum 4606 missing')
  if not all(('%s %d'%(k,val)) in _sec4 for k,val in _exp_codes.items()): err('section4 declared exclusion codes incomplete')
except Exception as e: err('section4 matrix: '+str(e))

# artifacts
def extract(name):
    marker='### '+name+' canonical block'
    i=text.index(marker)
    mstart=text.index('<!-- artifact:'+name+':start -->',i)
    astart=text.index('~~~text',mstart)+len('~~~text')
    # content begins after newline
    if text[astart:astart+1]=='\n': astart+=1
    aend=text.index('~~~',astart)
    content=text[astart:aend]
    if content.endswith('\n'): content=content[:-1]
    measured=re.search(r'\*\*Measured:\*\* (\d+) B / (\d+) LF / sha ([0-9a-f]{64})',text[i:aend])
    return content+'\n', measured
artfacts={}
for name in ['Crows-472.txt','Crows-472.json','README-472.md','MANIFEST-472.txt','preview-472.py','A-rows-blockA.txt','A-rows-blockB-r2.txt']:
    try: content,measured=extract(name)
    except Exception as e: err('artifact missing '+name+': '+str(e));continue
    b=content.encode();lf=b.count(b'\n');sha=hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
    if not measured: err('measured missing '+name);continue
    by,ml,ms=int(measured.group(1)),int(measured.group(2)),measured.group(3)
    if (b.__len__(),lf,sha)!=(by,ml,ms): err('artifact mismatch %s (%d,%d,%s) != (%d,%d,%s)'%(name,b.__len__(),lf,sha,by,ml,ms))
    artfacts[name]=(b,lf,sha)
# crows txt four rows
ct=artfacts.get('Crows-472.txt',(b'',0,''))[0].decode('utf-8')
if sum(1 for l in ct.split('\n') if l.startswith('| '))<4: err('Crows txt rows <4')
# crows json totals
try:
 cj=json.loads(artfacts['Crows-472.json'][0].decode('utf-8'))
 inv=cj.get('inventory',{})
 if inv.get('rows')!=len(rows): err('Crows.json rows mismatch')
 if inv.get('classes')!=dict(classes): err('Crows.json classes mismatch')
 if inv.get('dispositions')!=dict(disp): err('Crows.json disps mismatch')
 if inv.get('codes')!=dict(codes): err('Crows.json codes mismatch')
 if len(cj.get('rows',[]))!=4: err('Crows.json support rows !=4')
except Exception as e: err('Crows.json parse: '+str(e))
# support CLI actual run
try:
 tf=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(record)),'tmp-verify-472');os.makedirs(tf,exist_ok=True)
 open(os.path.join(tf,'preview-472.py'),'w',encoding='utf-8').write(artfacts['preview-472.py'][0].decode('utf-8'))
 open(os.path.join(tf,'Crows-472.json'),'w',encoding='utf-8').write(artfacts['Crows-472.json'][0].decode('utf-8'))
 out=subprocess.run([sys.executable,os.path.join(tf,'preview-472.py'),'query','acceptance'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=10)
 if out.returncode!=0: err('support-cli exit %d'%out.returncode)
 data=json.loads(out.stdout)
 if not data or 'apply' not in data[0]: err('support-cli apply key missing')
except Exception as e: err('support-cli: '+str(e))
try:
 shutil.rmtree(tf,ignore_errors=True)
except Exception: pass
# manifest references
try:
 man=artfacts['MANIFEST-472.txt'][0].decode('utf-8')
 for name in ['Crows-472.txt','Crows-472.json','README-472.md','preview-472.py','A-rows-blockA.txt','A-rows-blockB-r2.txt']:
  b,lf,sha=artfacts[name]
  if name not in man or sha not in man or str(len(b)) not in man: err('MANIFEST missing '+name)
except Exception as e: err('MANIFEST: '+str(e))
# row sources/stage-a
try:
 s8=pre.index('## 8. Stage C self account'); e8=pre.index('## 9.',s8)
 if len(re.findall(r'^<!-- Row source: agent=.*?-->\s*$',pre[s8:e8],re.M))!=4: err('row-source count !=4')
 _rs=re.findall(r'README=README-472.md sha256 ([0-9a-f]{64}) bytes=(\d+) lf=(\d+)',pre[s8:e8])
 if len(_rs)!=4: err('row-source README facts count %d !=4'%len(_rs))
 for _sha,_by,_lf in _rs:
  if _sha!=artfacts['README-472.md'][2] or int(_by)!=len(artfacts['README-472.md'][0]) or int(_lf)!=artfacts['README-472.md'][1]: err('row-source README facts differ from canonical README')
except ValueError as _e8: err('row-source section missing: '+str(_e8))
readme_sha=artfacts['README-472.md'][2]
if readme_sha not in pre: err('README hash not referenced')
if 'accepted paired-pilot plans' not in pre.lower(): err('stage-a pilot plans missing')
if 'two frozen plan accounts' not in pre: err('frozen plan accounts missing')
# stale markers
head=text.split('## 3.')[0]
for bad in ['5568615474','370d0278','two DCO','source-exhaustive proof']:
 if bad in head: err('stale marker '+bad)
# disk support
base=r'C:\src\Project Aegis\_A\evidence\read-lc-472-c1'
for name in ['Crows-472.txt','Crows-472.json','README-472.md','preview-472.py','A-rows-blockA.txt','A-rows-blockB-r2.txt','MANIFEST-472.txt']:
 p=os.path.join(base,name)
 if not os.path.exists(p): err('disk evidence missing '+name);continue
 d=open(p,'rb').read()
 if name in artfacts and hashlib.sha256(d).hexdigest()!=artfacts[name][2]: err('disk sha mismatch '+name)
# output
if errors:
 print('VERIFIER FAIL:');[print(' -',e) for e in errors];sys.exit(1)
print('VERIFIER OK rows=%d unique=%d malformed=%d classes=%s disps=%s exclusions=%d codes=%s per-page=16 Crows.json=full-totals+rows-agree raw-placeholders=%d inline-artifacts=7/7 stale=0 span=ok duplicate-token=ok support-cli=ok stage-a=ok row-source=ok crows-full=ok disk-support=ok stale-marker=ok page-totals=ok section4=ok'%(len(rows),len(keys),malformed,dict(classes),dict(disp),disp['excluded'],dict(codes),codes.get('PLACEHOLDER',0)))
~~~

## 11. Boundary and pilot reporting

Stage C only: one added record path shared by both blocks; no page edit; no plan/keeper/index/manifest/tool/test/workflow change; no merge; owner #848 untouched; not #805/#860/#866/#867. Candidate-token inventory is table-level self-consistency, never exact-once/source-exhaustive. **Pilot reporting (owner requirement):** this record is the Stage C implementation for the one owner-approved paired-implementation pilot (both plans accepted before C started; ONE record PR for both blocks); Stage C wall time and the factual audit-round comparison vs the last two single-block cycles (batch-25 and batch-26) are reported in the single SD-C completion comment and the final report, not self-measured inside this record. **Signed with both names - true identity: sd-c-read-lc-456-c3; stage role: sd-c-pilot-c1.** **Written by: DeepSeek V4 Flash (deepseek-flash, DeepSeek Harness).**
